Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program (Fernstudium)

(M.Sc.) Data Science (MADS-120 and MADS-60) (Start of studies up to and including: 02.03.2025)

in the 120 ECTS and 60 ECTS model:

  • a completed undergraduate degree program from a state or state-recognized university (of applied sciences) / university (may also have been completed abroad – verification via Anabin or Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 180 ECTS for the 120 ECTS Master program or with at least 240 ECTS for the 60 ECTS Master program. The subject area of the undergraduate degree program plays no role here.

  • a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master & MBA programs")

  • Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience after completion of the first undergraduate degree program (internships, trainee, or working student activities can be credited after individual review. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

    Professional experience from a successfully completed academically accompanied dual study program from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education Master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual study program matches the desired Master's degree program).

    If the professional experience cannot be demonstrated to the required extent: see Early Admission Master Programme - ONLY possible for the 120 ECTS model!)

  • The Master in Data Science can be completed in the 120 ECTS and in the 60 ECTS model in German or English. If the English-language variant is chosen, we require the corresponding proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR) (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.), see also the Leitfaden Sprachnachweise für die Zulassung zum Studium

In the 120 ECTS model:

In addition to the requirements, it applies that the study applicant must demonstrate the knowledge from the modules

  • BSTA – Statistik (Statistics)
  • IGIS – Grundlagen der industriellen Softwaretechnik (Fundamentals of Industrial Software Engineering)

PAGE 125 OF 245

. Should this proof not be able to be provided, the modules can be completed with us free of charge. For this purpose, the students are to be booked into a cohort with the two courses.

In the 60 ECTS model:

In addition to the requirements, it applies that the study applicant must demonstrate the knowledge from all modules

  • BSTA – Statistik (Statistics)
  • IGIS – Grundlagen der industriellen Softwaretechnik (Fundamentals of Industrial Software Engineering)
  • DLMDSAM01 – Advanced Mathematics (Weiterführende Mathematik)
  • DLMDSPWP01 – Programming with Python (Programmierung mit Python)

. Should this proof not be able to be provided, the modules can be completed with us free of charge. For this purpose, the students are to be booked into a cohort with the two courses.

Optional entry option for the 60 ECTS Master program (depending on prior education):

  • with 240 ECTS from the first degree program: direct access possible

  • with 210 ECTS from the first degree program: enrollment in courses amounting to 30 ECTS or proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program. (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. Professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

  • with 180 ECTS from the first degree program: enrollment in courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. Professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

Optional entry option to the continuing education Master program for applicants without a completed undergraduate degree program via the Master Eignungsprüfung (Master aptitude examination) (see Master for Professionals - Master Eignungsprüfung).

(This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree. Professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

  • with 180 ECTS from the first degree: completion of courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree. Professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

For admission to the English-language Master's program, the admission course "BSTE-02 Steuerlehre" does not have to be completed.

The Master in Finance, Accounting und Taxation can be completed in the 120 ECTS model in German or English. Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program

(M.Sc.) Industrial and Organizational Psychology (MAIOP-120-01)

in the 120 ECTS model:

Optional entry option for the consecutive 120 ECTS variant via the Master entrance examination

Assistance for admission:

  • Anyone who has a Bachelor's or Master's in economic sciences may not choose the 60 ECTS Master Industrial and Organizational Psychology (Admission is only possible for the 120 ECTS variant of the study program.)

  • Anyone who has a Bachelor's or Master's in Psychology, Applied Psychology or another area of psychology and fulfills the other conditions according to the AZE (ECTS, etc.) may do the 60 ECTS Master Industrial and Organizational Psychology, but not the 120 ECTS Master Industrial and Organizational Psychology.

  • with 240 ECTS from the first degree program: direct access possible

  • with 210 ECTS from the first degree program: taking courses amounting to 30 ECTS or proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program. (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. The professional experience from a Dual Study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

  • with 180 ECTS from the first degree program: taking courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. The professional experience from a Dual Study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

    Professional experience from a successfully completed, academically accompanied Dual Study program from Germany can be taken into account as proof of the qualified professional experience for a continuing education Master's degree program (if the subject-related focus of the practical professional activity in the Dual Study program matches the desired Master's degree program).

The professional experience from a Dual Study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

Special requirement for admission to the Master distance learning program

(M.A.) Management (MAMG-60)

in the 60 ECTS model:

  • a completed undergraduate degree program from a state or state-recognized (applied sciences) higher education institution / university (can also have been completed abroad – verification via Anabin or the Head of Guidelines Management for Matriculation and Recognition) with at least 240 ECTS. The subject area of the undergraduate degree program plays no role here.

  • a final grade of at least "Befriedigend" (Satisfactory) (3.5). (see further notes in the section "Supplement on the minimum grade for admission to Master's & MBA programs")

  • Proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience after completion of the first undergraduate degree program (internships, trainee, or working-student activities can be credited after individual review. The proof can be submitted informally as a confirmation from the employer.)

    Professional experience from a successfully completed, academically accompanied dual study program from Germany can be taken into account as proof of qualified professional experience for a continuing education Master's degree program (if the subject focus of the practical professional activity in the dual study program matches the desired Master's degree program).

  • Proof of English language skills for non-native speakers at least at level B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference (CEFR) (the proof must be submitted BEFORE the start of studies.), see also the guide Sprachnachweise für die Zulassung zum Studium (Language Certificates for Admission to Studies)

Optional entry option (depending on prior education):

  • with 240 ECTS from the first degree program: direct access possible

  • with 210 ECTS from the first degree program: completion of courses amounting to 30 ECTS or proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program. (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program. The professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

  • with 180 ECTS from the first degree program: completion of courses amounting to 30 ECTS and proof of at least one year of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.) or proof of at least two years of qualified professional experience at the start of the study program (This must be acquired after completion of the undergraduate degree program.

PAGE 189 OF 245

The professional experience from a dual study program cannot be used to bridge missing ECTS credit points.)

Optional entry option to the continuing education Master's program for applicants without a completed undergraduate degree program via the Master Eignungsprüfung (Master aptitude examination) (see Master for Professionals - Master Eignungsprüfung).

ADMISSION OF APPLICANTS WITH A LEGAL STAATSEXAMEN (STATE EXAMINATION IN LAW)

Applicants with a passed first legal Staatsexamen (standard period of study: 8 semesters plus first Staatsexamen) can be admitted to Master's degree programs. The final grade is not relevant for the admission.

If a failure of the first legal state examination (Staatsprüfung) exists, likewise no completed degree exists.

Note:

A special feature is the Mannheim Model (Mannheimer-Modell): Here, after 6 semesters, the LL.B. (Unternehmensjurist/in) is earned. This corresponds to a normal BA with 180 ECTS. In addition, following this, after a further 4 semesters, the first Staatsexamen can be taken.

ADMISSION OF APPLICANTS WITH A SECOND MEDICAL STAATSEXAMEN (STATE EXAMINATION IN MEDICINE)

Applicants with a passed second medical Staatsexamen (standard period of study: 10 semesters plus first Staatsexamen plus practical year) can be admitted to Master's degree programs. The final grade is not relevant for the admission.

The practical year between the first and second medical Staatsexamen can already be taken into account as professional experience.

PAGE 237 OF 245

Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung (Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition) — iu Internationale Hochschule

DOCUMENTS TO BE SUBMITTED FOR MA DEGREE PROGRAMS

Applicants for a Master's degree program must submit the following documents:

ALL documents required for the proof of admission (e.g. certificate with the academic degree or the deed with which the academic degree is conferred, as well as the "Transcript of records", from which the successful completion of studies with overall grade and ECTS is unambiguously evident, in officially certified copy (on this, see "die Beglaubigung" (the certification)))

Note: For foreign-language certificates or attestations that are not written in English, a German-language translation must be enclosed, the correctness of which has been certified by the competent German diplomatic or consular representation in the country of origin or by a sworn interpreter/translator in the Federal Republic of Germany.

  • Tabular curriculum vitae

  • Copy of the identity card or passport. The copy of the identity document for identification purposes is only permissible if all data fields, including the photograph, are blacked out, with the exception of: first name, surname, date of birth and nationality.

  • De-registration certificate (Exmatrikulationsbescheinigung) in the case of enrollment in a Master's degree program at another university that has already taken place

Please note: The student here has the obligation to furnish (Bringschuld) and is responsible for submitting all documents completely and correctly. Subsequent submissions of documents are only to be accepted in exceptional cases and are to be coordinated with the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition.

Matriculation only takes place when all documents for the determination of the HZB are available.

In the EAO it furthermore states: The examination board or a person commissioned by it decides at its own discretion on the acceptance of other proofs. In this case, the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition. After a positive review of the higher education admission qualification (Hochschulzulassungsberechtigung) by the responsible administrative clerk, written information is sent to the applicant and the matriculation takes place. In the case of a negative review of the higher education admission qualification, the applicant is likewise informed in writing and promptly about the rejection, and the documents submitted in the original are sent back. Copies are destroyed after a period of 3 months. The copy of the identity card is destroyed after successful identity verification.

These steps must be urgently observed and complied with!

PAGE 238 OF 245

Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung (Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition) — iu Internationale Hochschule

MATRICULATION SUBJECT TO RESERVATION IN THE MASTER'S PROGRAM

Applicants who have indeed completed the Bachelor's degree and submit a corresponding proof, but have not yet received a deed (Urkunde) and thus also cannot yet present one, must additionally present from their university a proof of the successfully completed undergraduate degree with the final grade. Up to the point in time at which the deed is available, the applicants are matriculated "subject to reservation" ("unter Vorbehalt").

PAGE 239 OF 245

THE OFFICIAL CERTIFICATION

Official certification can be performed by public bodies that hold an official seal (Dienstsiegel).

These are in particular:

  • Public authorities, e.g. municipal, district, city administration (town hall), rural districts
  • Lower administrative authorities such as local mayors and village heads
  • Offices, e.g. district administration office, tax office, employment agency (Agentur für Arbeit)
  • Notaries
  • Churches organized under public law
  • Police and courts
  • Bundeswehr (German Armed Forces)
  • Chambers of crafts (Handwerkskammern)
  • Student services organizations (Studierendenwerke)

(certified copies are also recognized if the certification is prepared and stamped by the body that also issued the original, e.g. schools, universities)

Documents can also be verified free of charge at our Dual Campuses of the IU (Duale Standorten der IU). All that is required for this is a copy and the original of the document.

The official certification must, as the sample here shows, contain at least:

  1. A note (Vermerk) certifying that the copy corresponds to the original. Such as, for example, "It is hereby officially certified that the copy on the front/reverse corresponds to the original" (certification note).

  2. The signature of the certifying person

  3. The imprint of the official seal. An official seal generally contains an emblem.

If the copy consists of several individual sheets, it must be proven that each page originates from the same document. It is sufficient if only one page bears the certification note and the

PAGE 240 OF 245

signature, provided all sheets are laid on top of one another, stapled, and sealed in such a way that a part of the official seal imprint appears on each page (see illustration in the upper left part of the sample). If the certification does not satisfy the requirements, the IU cannot recognize the proof. The student bears the obligation to deliver (Bringschuld) here!

Not recognized are certifications from the following bodies:

  • Lawyers
  • Associations
  • Auditors or accountants
  • Health insurance funds (Krankenkassen)*
  • Savings banks and banks

* INTERNAL EXCEPTION RULE

(no communication to students):

Internally, we also recognize the certification of health insurance funds (BKK´s, IKK´S, EK´s and Knappschaften). The AOK is excluded and is not recognized.

The health insurance funds whose certifications we accept have a corresponding official seal — usually with the federal eagle (Bundesadler).

Example:

In case of ambiguities, please always contact the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition (Leitung Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung)!

PAGE 241 OF 245

Certifications from abroad

Certifications from abroad can be prepared by the following bodies:

  • German embassies and consulates

  • The school or university that issued the certificates. It may certify copies of its own certificates. In this case, the certifications are to be performed by the head of the school or by the dean or rectorate of the university — provided with the official seal. A certification by the secretariat is not sufficient.

  • A notary who provides the copies with the Apostille stamp (does not apply to EU countries).

  • The ministry of education (Kultusministerium) of the respective country

  • Public bodies that hold a corresponding seal (courts and public authorities)

We do not accept certifications without a seal!

In the German legal sphere (DACH), the prognosis decision within the right of access is fundamentally present. I.e. certifications from Austria and Switzerland can be recognized as equivalent.

On the basis of the bilateral agreement, an Apostille can fundamentally be dispensed with for documents from member states of the EU.

Translation of the documents

If the qualification was issued in a language other than English or German, we require a translation by a sworn translator.

Such translators can certify their own translations, but not the original qualification!

Should there be doubts here, please always consult with the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition (Leitung Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung).

PAGE 242 OF 245

VALIDATION OF EMPLOYMENT REFERENCES/EMPLOYER CONFIRMATIONS

It is not formally legally required that an employment reference (Arbeitszeugnis)/an employer confirmation (Arbeitgeberbestätigung) be validated by means of a company stamp; merely a signature is required in order to fulfill the evidentiary function of an employment reference in legal transactions and to be able to identify the issuer of the reference. In the course of quality assurance in the form of verification or plausibility checking, attention must be paid to the following criteria, which can provide an indication that the submitted employment reference (employer confirmation) is authentic:

  • Company logo, address, contact details

  • Identification of the document issuer by means of

    • handwritten signature or company stamp or electronic seal
    • alternatively, a qualified electronic signature in accordance with the Regulation of EIDAS can also be used, or other official international regulations or official digital certificates whose signatures originate from public authorities

  • If available, verification by blockchain or employment references / employer confirmations with codes for verification

  • A detailed description of tasks is not required in the employment reference / in the employer confirmation. Information from which the job title / the occupational designation / the occupational field emerges, as well as the duration (exact date) of the employment relationship, is sufficient

  • With regard to the proof of the required scope of employment, full-time employment can always be assumed ex silencio, since working hours amounting to at least 80% are no longer mentioned as anything other than full-time in the reference.

  • Part-time employment relationships, on the other hand, must be stated exactly. Working hours under 32 hours per week count as part-time

  • Marginal employment relationships (Geringfügige Beschäftigungsverhältnisse) and mini-jobs cannot be taken into account as proof of the required professional experience

PAGE 243 OF 245

SECONDARY AUDITORS (ZWEITHÖRER)

Enrolled students of other higher education institutions who are not on leave of absence can be admitted as secondary auditors (Zweithörer) with the entitlement to attend courses and to take examinations accompanying their studies, insofar as no restrictions exist. This is regulated by our AZE.

I.e. with us, secondary auditors can fundamentally be enrolled.

Dual students cannot enroll in distance learning in the same degree program as secondary auditors.

Should there be any ambiguities here, please always contact the Head of Guideline Management for Matriculation and Recognition (Leitung Richtlinienmanagement für Immatrikulation und Anerkennung).
